# Git rails test audit

One row per named source case and explicit unittest method; selected classes may run the same source case both against the core and through the hook. Unrated rows are explicitly marked. This does not expand dynamic loops in methods into separate rows. Lines reflect the inspected worktree; removed rows use their former positions.

Selector compatibility: removed or renamed test names no longer select their former tests. The existing selector exits successfully when it selects zero tests; this behavior was not changed or independently validated as a public contract.

| 768 | SlowGitRead | test_hanging_git_read_is_limit_exceeded | <code>def test_hanging_git_read_is_limit_exceeded(self) -&gt; None:         fake = self.probe / &#x27;bin/git&#x27;         fake.write_text(&#x27;#!/bin/bash\n&#x27;                         &#x27;case &quot; $* &quot; in *&quot; rev-parse &quot;*) sleep 30 ;; esac\n&#x27;                         f&#x27;exec {shlex.quote(REAL_GIT)} &quot;$@&quot;\n&#x27;)         fake.chmod(0o755)         self.environ[&#x27;PATH&#x27;] = f&#x27;{self.probe / &quot;bin&quot;}{os.pathsep}{self.environ[&quot;PATH&quot;]}&#x27;         started = time.monotonic()         decision = self.decide(self.event(&#x27;git add a.txt&#x27;, str(self.project)))         elapsed = time.monotonic() - started         self.assertEqual(decision[:2], (&#x27;deny&#x27;, &#x27;limit-exceeded&#x27;))         self.assertGreaterEqual(elapsed, 1.5, &#x27;the read limit is two seconds&#x27;)         self.assertLess(elapsed, 4.5, &#x27;a timed-out read must be stopped, including its children&#x27;)</code> | 85 | Injects a hanging Git read and checks the time limit and termination. |
| 804 | GitReadIsolation | test_git_reads_are_sanitized | <code>def test_git_reads_are_sanitized(self) -&gt; None:         log = self.install_recorders()         resolution = self.resolve(&#x27;git -C sub status; git p&#x27;, str(self.project))         self.assertEqual(tuple(item.repository for item in resolution.invocations),                          (str(self.project), str(self.project)))         calls = log.read_text().splitlines()         self.assertTrue(calls, &#x27;resolution must read the effective repository&#x27;)         for call in calls:             self.assertTrue(call.startswith(&#x27;locks=0 prompt=0 dir=unset tree=unset index=unset common=unset &#x27;), call)             self.assertIn(&#x27;-c core.fsmonitor=false&#x27;, call)</code> | 90 | Checks sanitized read environment and forbids network operations. |

| 1575 | SlowRuleRead | test_hanging_staging_read_is_limit_exceeded | <code>def test_hanging_staging_read_is_limit_exceeded(self) -&gt; None:         fake = self.probe / &#x27;bin/git&#x27;         fake.write_text(&#x27;#!/bin/bash\n&#x27;                         &#x27;case &quot; $* &quot; in *&quot; ls-files &quot;*) sleep 30 ;; esac\n&#x27;                         f&#x27;exec {shlex.quote(REAL_GIT)} &quot;$@&quot;\n&#x27;)         fake.chmod(0o755)         self.environ[&#x27;PATH&#x27;] = f&#x27;{self.probe / &quot;bin&quot;}{os.pathsep}{self.environ[&quot;PATH&quot;]}&#x27;         started = time.monotonic()         decision = self.decide(self.event(&#x27;git add missing.txt&#x27;, str(self.project)))         elapsed = time.monotonic() - started         self.assertEqual(decision[:2], (&#x27;deny&#x27;, &#x27;limit-exceeded&#x27;))         self.assertLess(elapsed, 4.5, &#x27;a timed-out read must be stopped, including its children&#x27;)</code> | 85 | Injects a hanging rule read and checks bounded failure. |

| 2313 | PushReadIsolation | test_push_decisions_never_run_network_git_or_gh | <code>def test_push_decisions_never_run_network_git_or_gh(self) -&gt; None:         self.setup_push_extra_pattern()         self.set_config(&#x27;remote.web.url&#x27;, &#x27;https://example.invalid/r.git&#x27;)         log = self.probe / &#x27;calls.log&#x27;         git = self.probe / &#x27;bin/git&#x27;         git.write_text(&#x27;#!/bin/bash\n&#x27;                        &#x27;printf &quot;locks=%s prompt=%s dir=%s args=%s\\n&quot; &quot;${GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS-unset}&quot; &#x27;                        f&#x27;&quot;${{GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT-unset}}&quot; &quot;${{GIT_DIR-unset}}&quot; &quot;$*&quot; &gt;&gt; {shlex.quote(str(log))}\n&#x27;                        f&#x27;exec {shlex.quote(REAL_GIT)} &quot;$@&quot;\n&#x27;)         git.chmod(0o755)         self.environ[&#x27;PATH&#x27;] = f&#x27;{self.probe / &quot;bin&quot;}{os.pathsep}{self.environ[&quot;PATH&quot;]}&#x27;         self.environ[&#x27;GIT_DIR&#x27;] = str(self.outside / &#x27;.git&#x27;)         for command in (&#x27;git push&#x27;, &#x27;git push origin feat/x:stable/1&#x27;, &#x27;git push web feat/x&#x27;, &#x27;git push -f&#x27;,                         &#x27;git push --force-with-lease origin feat/x&#x27;, &#x27;git push origin v1 :feat/y&#x27;,                         &#x27;git push https://example.invalid/r.git main&#x27;, &#x27;git push --repo=origin --tags&#x27;):             with self.subTest(command=command):                 self.assertNotEqual(self.decide(self.event(command, str(self.project)))[0], &#x27;not_applicable&#x27;)         calls = log.read_text().splitlines()         self.assertTrue(calls, &#x27;push decisions must read the repository&#x27;)         for call in calls:             self.assertTrue(call.startswith(&#x27;locks=0 prompt=0 dir=unset &#x27;), call)             arguments = call.split(&#x27;args=&#x27;, 1)[1].split()             for network in NETWORK_SUBCOMMANDS:                 self.assertNotIn(network, arguments, call)</code> | 90 | Checks push decisions use sanitized local reads and do not invoke network commands. |

| 2966 | OperationReadIsolation | test_operation_decisions_never_run_network_git_or_gh | <code>def test_operation_decisions_never_run_network_git_or_gh(self) -&gt; None:         self.setup_push_extra_pattern()         log = self.probe / &#x27;calls.log&#x27;         git = self.probe / &#x27;bin/git&#x27;         git.write_text(&#x27;#!/bin/bash\n&#x27;                        &#x27;printf &quot;locks=%s prompt=%s dir=%s args=%s\\n&quot; &quot;${GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS-unset}&quot; &#x27;                        f&#x27;&quot;${{GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT-unset}}&quot; &quot;${{GIT_DIR-unset}}&quot; &quot;$*&quot; &gt;&gt; {shlex.quote(str(log))}\n&#x27;                        f&#x27;exec {shlex.quote(REAL_GIT)} &quot;$@&quot;\n&#x27;)         git.chmod(0o755)         gh = self.probe / &#x27;bin/gh&#x27;         gh.write_text(f&#x27;#!/bin/bash\nprintf &quot;gh %s\\n&quot; &quot;$*&quot; &gt;&gt; {shlex.quote(str(log))}\n&#x27;)         gh.chmod(0o755)         self.environ[&#x27;PATH&#x27;] = f&#x27;{self.probe / &quot;bin&quot;}{os.pathsep}{self.environ[&quot;PATH&quot;]}&#x27;         self.environ[&#x27;GIT_DIR&#x27;] = str(self.outside / &#x27;.git&#x27;)         for command in (&#x27;git merge main&#x27;, &#x27;git pull&#x27;, &#x27;git pull --ff-only origin main&#x27;, &#x27;git rebase main&#x27;,                         &#x27;git rebase --continue&#x27;, &#x27;git branch -m feat/z&#x27;, &#x27;git branch -f stable/b&#x27;,                         &#x27;git update-ref -d refs/heads/feat/y&#x27;, &#x27;git checkout -B feat/y&#x27;, &#x27;git reset --hard&#x27;,                         &#x27;git clean -fd&#x27;, &#x27;gh pr merge 1&#x27;, &#x27;gh api -X PUT repos/o/r/pulls/1/merge&#x27;):             with self.subTest(command=command):                 self.assertNotEqual(self.decide(self.event(command, str(self.project)))[0], &#x27;not_applicable&#x27;)         calls = log.read_text().splitlines()         self.assertFalse([call for call in calls if call.startswith(&#x27;gh &#x27;)], &#x27;the core never runs gh&#x27;)         for call in calls:             self.assertTrue(call.startswith(&#x27;locks=0 prompt=0 dir=unset &#x27;), call)             arguments = call.split(&#x27;args=&#x27;, 1)[1].split()             for network in NETWORK_SUBCOMMANDS:                 self.assertNotIn(network, arguments, call)</code> | 90 | Checks operation decisions use sanitized local reads without network commands. |

| 3720 | RestackVerifierInterface | test_lease_request_carries_each_destination_source | <code>def test_lease_request_carries_each_destination_source(self) -&gt; None:         self.setup_restack_replayed_on_a()         self.write_journals()         calls = self.recorder(&#x27;LEASE_VERIFIER&#x27;, True)         lease = f&#x27;--force-with-lease=refs/heads/feat/a:{self.shas[&quot;a_remote&quot;]}&#x27;         both = f&#x27;{lease} --force-with-lease=refs/heads/feat/b:&#x27;         for refspecs, sources in ((&#x27;origin feat/a&#x27;, (&#x27;feat/a&#x27;,)), (&#x27;origin main~0:feat/a&#x27;, (&#x27;main~0&#x27;,)),                                   (&#x27;origin refs/heads/feat/a:feat/a&#x27;, (&#x27;refs/heads/feat/a&#x27;,)),                                   (&#x27;origin HEAD&#x27;, (&#x27;refs/heads/feat/a&#x27;,))):             with self.subTest(refspecs=refspecs):                 calls.clear()                 self.outcome(f&#x27;git push {lease} {refspecs}&#x27;)                 self.assertEqual(tuple(getattr(calls[0][0], &#x27;sources&#x27;, ())), sources)         calls.clear()         self.outcome(f&#x27;git push {both} origin feat/a feat/b&#x27;)         request = calls[0][0]         self.assertEqual((tuple(request.destinations), tuple(getattr(request, &#x27;sources&#x27;, ()))),                          ((&#x27;refs/heads/feat/a&#x27;, &#x27;refs/heads/feat/b&#x27;), (&#x27;feat/a&#x27;, &#x27;feat/b&#x27;)))</code> | 90 | Checks shipped verifiers, journal decisions, request details, and deterministic failures. |
| 3739 | RestackVerifierInterface | test_verifier_failure_is_named_in_the_decision | <code>def test_verifier_failure_is_named_in_the_decision(self) -&gt; None:         self.setup_restack_replayed()         self.write_journals()         self.recorder(&#x27;LEASE_VERIFIER&#x27;, True)         self.recorder(&#x27;REPLAY_VERIFIER&#x27;, True)         self.assertEqual(self.outcome(PUSH_A.format(**self.shas)), (&#x27;ask&#x27;, LEASE, &#x27;R18&#x27;, &#x27;fixture verifier refused&#x27;))         self.assertEqual(self.outcome(&#x27;git rebase main&#x27;),                          (&#x27;ask&#x27;, OUTSIDE_RESTACK, &#x27;R26&#x27;, &#x27;fixture verifier refused&#x27;))</code> | 90 | Checks shipped verifiers, journal decisions, request details, and deterministic failures. |
| 3748 | RestackVerifierInterface | test_no_journal_decisions_name_the_missing_journal | <code>def test_no_journal_decisions_name_the_missing_journal(self) -&gt; None:         self.setup_push_feature()         self.assertEqual(self.outcome(f&#x27;git push --force-with-lease=feat/x:{LEASE_SHA} origin feat/x&#x27;),                          (&#x27;ask&#x27;, LEASE, &#x27;R18&#x27;, &#x27;journal-missing&#x27;))         self.assertEqual(self.outcome(&#x27;git rebase main&#x27;), (&#x27;ask&#x27;, OUTSIDE_RESTACK, &#x27;R26&#x27;, &#x27;journal-missing&#x27;))</code> | 90 | Checks shipped verifiers, journal decisions, request details, and deterministic failures. |

| 3765 | RestackVerifierInterface | test_protected_destination_with_a_valid_journal_names_the_protected_check | <code>def test_protected_destination_with_a_valid_journal_names_the_protected_check(self) -&gt; None:         self.setup_restack_replayed()         self.edit_journal((&#x27;order&#x27;,), (&#x27;main&#x27;, &#x27;feat/a&#x27;, &#x27;feat/b&#x27;))         self.edit_journal((&#x27;branches&#x27;, &#x27;main&#x27;), MAIN_REPLAYED)         self.write_journals()         calls = self.recorder(&#x27;LEASE_VERIFIER&#x27;, False)         command = f&#x27;git push --force-with-lease=refs/heads/main:{self.shas[&quot;a_base&quot;]} origin main&#x27;         self.assertEqual(self.outcome(command)[:2], (&#x27;ask&#x27;, PROTECTED))         self.assertEqual((calls[0][1].verified, calls[0][1].failure), (False, &#x27;branch-protected&#x27;))</code> | 90 | Checks shipped verifiers, journal decisions, request details, and deterministic failures. |

| 4727 | EntrypointFastPath | test_missing_registry_exits_silently_without_python | <code>def test_missing_registry_exits_silently_without_python(self) -&gt; None:         self.registry.unlink()         for command in (&#x27;git push -f&#x27;, &#x27;git reset --hard&#x27;, &#x27;gh pr merge 1&#x27;, &#x27;git add -A&#x27;):             with self.subTest(command=command):                 run = self.hook_twice(self.payload(command), self.without_python())                 self.assertEqual(run.output, (0, b&#x27;&#x27;, b&#x27;&#x27;))</code> | 90 | Checks early hook startup, isolation, or fail-closed behavior with the indicated environment. |
| 4734 | EntrypointFastPath | test_command_without_git_word_exits_silently_without_python | <code>def test_command_without_git_word_exits_silently_without_python(self) -&gt; None:         for command in (&#x27;ls -la&#x27;, &#x27;bash hooks/git-rails.sh&#x27;, &#x27;cat .git/config&#x27;, &#x27;curl https://github.com/o/r&#x27;,                         &#x27;echo legit digits ghost gh-pages git_x .gh&#x27;, &#x27;&#x27;):             with self.subTest(command=command):                 run = self.hook_twice(self.payload(command, self.location(&#x27;clean&#x27;)), self.without_python())                 self.assertEqual(run.output, (0, b&#x27;&#x27;, b&#x27;&#x27;))</code> | 90 | Checks early hook startup, isolation, or fail-closed behavior with the indicated environment. |

| 4764 | EntrypointFastPath | test_python_older_than_3_11_is_runtime_unavailable | <code>def test_python_older_than_3_11_is_runtime_unavailable(self) -&gt; None:         shim_directory = self.probe / &#x27;old python&#x27;         shim_directory.mkdir()         log = self.probe / &#x27;old-python.log&#x27;         shim = shim_directory / &#x27;python3&#x27;         shim.write_text(&#x27;#!/bin/sh\n&#x27;                         f&#x27;printf &quot;%s\\n&quot; &quot;$*&quot; &gt;&gt; {shlex.quote(str(log))}\n&#x27;                         &#x27;case &quot;$1&quot; in --version&#124;-V) echo &quot;Python 3.10.12&quot;; exit 0 ;; esac\n&#x27;                         &#x27;exit 1\n&#x27;)         shim.chmod(0o755)         environ = {**self.environ, &#x27;PATH&#x27;: f&#x27;{shim_directory}{os.pathsep}{python_free_path()}&#x27;}         run = self.hook_twice(self.payload(&#x27;git add a.txt&#x27;), environ)         self.assert_protocol(run, Expected(&#x27;deny&#x27;, &#x27;runtime-unavailable&#x27;), environ)         calls = log.read_text() if log.exists() else &#x27;&#x27;         self.assertNotIn(&#x27;git_rails.py&#x27;, calls, &#x27;an interpreter older than 3.11 never runs the core&#x27;)</code> | 90 | Checks early hook startup, isolation, or fail-closed behavior with the indicated environment. |
| 4780 | EntrypointFastPath | test_core_runs_isolated_from_python_environment_variables | <code>def test_core_runs_isolated_from_python_environment_variables(self) -&gt; None:         planted = self.probe / &#x27;planted&#x27;         planted.mkdir()         marker = self.probe / &#x27;planted-module-ran&#x27;         body = f&#x27;open({str(marker)!r}, &quot;w&quot;).close()\nraise SystemExit(0)\n&#x27;         for name in (&#x27;sitecustomize.py&#x27;, &#x27;usercustomize.py&#x27;, &#x27;json.py&#x27;, &#x27;re.py&#x27;, &#x27;dataclasses.py&#x27;):             (planted / name).write_text(body)         environ = {**self.environ, &#x27;PYTHONPATH&#x27;: str(planted), &#x27;PYTHONHOME&#x27;: str(planted),                    &#x27;PYTHONSTARTUP&#x27;: str(planted / &#x27;json.py&#x27;)}         self.assertEqual(self.decide(self.event(&#x27;git add -A&#x27;, self.location(&#x27;project&#x27;)), environ),                          (&#x27;deny&#x27;, &#x27;blanket-staging&#x27;, &#x27;R01&#x27;))         self.assertFalse(marker.exists(), &#x27;a module from the hook environment ran inside the hook&#x27;)</code> | 90 | Checks early hook startup, isolation, or fail-closed behavior with the indicated environment. |
| 4793 | EntrypointFastPath | test_entrypoint_is_a_strict_bash_script_with_a_header | <code>def test_entrypoint_is_a_strict_bash_script_with_a_header(self) -&gt; None:         self.assertTrue(HOOK_PATH.is_file(), &#x27;hooks/git-rails.sh must exist&#x27;)         text = HOOK_PATH.read_text(encoding=&#x27;utf-8&#x27;)         lines = text.splitlines()         self.assertIn(lines[0], (&#x27;#!/usr/bin/env bash&#x27;, &#x27;#!/bin/bash&#x27;))         header: list[str] = []         for line in lines[1:]:             if not line.startswith(&#x27;#&#x27;):                 break             header.append(line.lower())         for word in (&#x27;contract&#x27;, &#x27;usage&#x27;, &#x27;exit&#x27;):             self.assertIn(word, &#x27;\n&#x27;.join(header), &#x27;the header comment names the &#x27; + word)         self.assertIn(&#x27;set -uo pipefail&#x27;, lines)         self.assertNotRegex(text, r&#x27;\bjq\b&#x27;)         self.assertNotIn(RULE_PATH_TEXT, text, &#x27;the rule line is read from the core constant, not copied&#x27;)         self.assertNotRegex(text, PIPELINE_IDENTIFIER)         checked = subprocess.run([BASH, &#x27;-n&#x27;, str(HOOK_PATH)], capture_output=True, text=True)         self.assertEqual(checked.returncode, 0, checked.stderr)</code> | 90 | Checks early hook startup, isolation, or fail-closed behavior with the indicated environment. |

| 4957 | EntrypointDiagnostics | test_secrets_never_reach_the_hook_output | <code>def test_secrets_never_reach_the_hook_output(self) -&gt; None:         self.setup_push_feature()         (self.project / &#x27;secret-msg.txt&#x27;).write_text(&#x27;Add parser SECRET-FILE-7f3\n\nRefs T-04\n&#x27;)         environ = {**self.environ, &#x27;GH_TOKEN&#x27;: &#x27;SECRET-HOOK-ENV-91c&#x27;}         cases = ((f&quot;git commit -m &#x27;Subject SECRET-MSG-2a1&#x27; -m &#x27;{TRAILER}&#x27;&quot;,                   Expected(&#x27;deny&#x27;, &#x27;commit-message-forbidden&#x27;, &#x27;R09&#x27;)),                  (&#x27;git commit -F secret-msg.txt&#x27;, Expected(&#x27;deny&#x27;, &#x27;commit-message-forbidden&#x27;, &#x27;R09&#x27;)),                  (&#x27;API_TOKEN=SECRET-ASSIGN-5d2 git push -f origin feat/x&#x27;, Expected(&#x27;ask&#x27;, &#x27;push-force&#x27;, &#x27;R17&#x27;)),                  (&#x27;git push https://URLUSER-6b4:SECRET-URL-8e0@example.invalid/r.git feat/x&#x27;,                   Expected(&#x27;deny&#x27;, &#x27;push-target-unknown&#x27;, &#x27;R12&#x27;)))         for command, expected in cases:             with self.subTest(command=command):                 run = self.hook_twice(self.payload(command), environ)                 self.assert_protocol(run, expected, environ)                 output = run.stdout + run.stderr                 for secret in (b&#x27;SECRET-&#x27;, b&#x27;URLUSER-&#x27;, b&#x27;Co-authored-by&#x27;, b&#x27;T-04&#x27;, command.encode()):                     self.assertNotIn(secret, output)</code> | 90 | Checks that hook diagnostics are complete, bounded, or free of private data. |

| 5262 | CoreEntryPoint | test_budget_signal_inside_the_failure_handler_still_denies | <code>def test_budget_signal_inside_the_failure_handler_still_denies(self) -&gt; None:         main, budget, denial = self.api(&#x27;main&#x27;), self.api(&#x27;BudgetExceeded&#x27;), self.api(&#x27;denial&#x27;)          def failing_decide(event: object, environ: Mapping[str, str]) -&gt; Any:             raise RuntimeError(&#x27;fault inside the decision&#x27;)          def late_denial(reason: str, rule: str &#124; None = None) -&gt; Any:             if reason == &#x27;internal-error&#x27;:                 raise budget()  # the budget signal arriving while the failure is handled             return denial(reason, rule)          self.patch(&#x27;decide&#x27;, failing_decide)         self.patch(&#x27;denial&#x27;, late_denial)         self.addCleanup(signal.signal, signal.SIGALRM, signal.getsignal(signal.SIGALRM))         stdout, stderr = io.BytesIO(), io.BytesIO()         for name, stream in ((&#x27;stdin&#x27;, io.BytesIO(b&#x27;{}&#x27;)), (&#x27;stdout&#x27;, stdout), (&#x27;stderr&#x27;, stderr)):             self.addCleanup(setattr, sys, name, getattr(sys, name))             setattr(sys, name, SimpleNamespace(buffer=stream))         try:             status = main()         except BaseException as error:  # noqa: B902 - the escape itself is the failure under test             self.fail(f&#x27;{type(error).__name__} escaped the core entry point&#x27;)         headline = stderr.getvalue().decode(&#x27;utf-8&#x27;).split(&#x27;\n&#x27;)[0]         self.assertEqual((status, stdout.getvalue()), (2, b&#x27;&#x27;))         self.assertIn(headline, (&#x27;git-rails: deny limit-exceeded (none)&#x27;, &#x27;git-rails: deny internal-error (none)&#x27;))</code> | 90 | Injects a budget signal during error handling and requires a fail-closed result. |
